Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the latin space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 273 and the convergence rate is O(813/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to latin structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 273/813 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
